- **go-pr-reviewer**: Dispatch when a Go pull request, diff, patch, or set of staged changes needs a community-style review before merge. - **goal-run-implementer**: Implements one iteration of a locked goal plan for goal-run.ts, test-first, inside the paths its gate block declares. Cannot commit, push, stage or tick a checkbox. - **goal-run-lens**: Refutes the iteration(s) goal-run.ts just landed and returns one-sentence verdicts with file:line anchors. Read-only, advisory, never blocks a run. Never requests changes. - **gpt-chat**: Conducts a conversation with a GPT model (Codex) as a separate conversational partner—the thread persists between messages, and the model remembers previous turns. ([VlDubr/tandem](https://github.com/VlDubr/tandem/blob/HEAD/agents/gpt-chat.md) · ★2)
- **gpt-delegate**: Assigns a task to a GPT model (Codex) for execution and monitors the background job through completion. ([VlDubr/tandem](https://github.com/VlDubr/tandem/blob/HEAD/agents/gpt-delegate.md) · ★2)
- **gpt-luna-low**: Perform cheap mechanical cross-family work or summaries; verify consequential output with a stronger route. ([hughescr/claude-code-config](https://github.com/hughescr/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/agents/gpt-luna-low.md) · ★1)
- **gpt-luna-medium**: Perform bounded cross-family work with objective checks; keep the input short, because this model's long-context recall is weak. ([hughescr/claude-code-config](https://github.com/hughescr/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/agents/gpt-luna-medium.md) · ★1)
- **gpt-researcher**: async def run_research(): researcher = GPTResearcher( query="Evolution of agentic frameworks in early 2027", report_type="research_report", tone="technical" ) await researcher.conduct_research() report = await researcher.write_report() return report ``` ([joanmarcriera/Home-office-automations](https://github.com/joanmarcriera/Home-office-automations/blob/HEAD/docs/tools/agents/gpt-researcher.md) · ★5)
- **gpt-review**: GPT-6 Astra で動くレビュー専用エージェント。claudep / claudex で起動したセッションでしか使えない （素の claude では model が Anthropic に弾かれる）。コード・設計・プラン・仕様書など成果物を問わずレビューし、 指摘と改善提案だけを返す。修正はしない。 呼び出し元は目的・制約・対象ファイルパス・必読資料パスを全て渡すこと。 修正後の再レビューは同じエージェントに SendMessage で修正内容を送る（会話が残るので前回指摘との突き合わせができる）。 確証バイアスを避けたい最終確認は、前回の指摘を渡さずに新しいインスタンスを起動する。 ([tak848/dotfiles](https://github.com/tak848/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_claude/agents/gpt-review.md) · ★3)
